Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The medium variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 798 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 314 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by medium generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 798, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 314. QED.
